### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCP と UDP の一番の違いは、**TCP は届いたかどうかを確認しながら確実に届けるのに対し、UDP は確認なしで速く送る**ことです。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para ordenar tus commits locales antes de compartirlos, porque deja el historial lineal. Usa merge para integrar una rama que ya subiste o que otros usan, porque no reescribe el historial.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、面向连接的，保证数据按序、不丢不重地到达；UDP 则不保证，速度快、开销小，但丢了就丢了。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own local branch to tidy its commits before you share it, so the history stays linear. Merge when the branch is already shared or you want the record of when the work was combined.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接把文件路径跟在 `bun test` 后面就行：

```bash
bun test test/utils.test.ts
```

如果不记得完整路径，也可以写文件名的一部分，Bun 会匹配名字里包含这段文字的测试文件：

```bash
bun test utils
```

要只跑某个测试用例，用 `-t` 按名字过滤：

```bash
bun test utils -t "解析日期"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where every change to a database gets written down before it's applied to the actual data. If the system crashes midway, it can replay that log to recover exactly what was committed and throw away what wasn't.
```

(function () {
  var list = document.getElementById('list');

  var updated = document.getElementById('updated');
  function esc(s) {
    return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) {
      return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c];
    });

    if (!trains.length) {
      count.innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">没有</span>';
      list.innerHTML = '<div class="k-empty">目前没有 600 元以下的班次</div>';
      return;
    }
    count.textContent = trains.length + ' 班';
    list.innerHTML = trains.map(function (t) {
      return '<div class="k-row"><div class="k-row-main">' + esc(t.no) +
        '</div><div class="k-row-sub">二等座余 ' + esc(t.left) + ' 张</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-value">¥' + esc(t.price) + '</div></div>';
    }).join('');
  }
  render(ducter.get('trains'));
  ducter.onChange(render);
})();
</script>

  var seed = ducter.get("seed") || [];

  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('runs')).then(function (v) {
    load(v == null ? seed : v);
    if (v == null) ducter.set('runs', seed);
  });
  ducter.onChange(function () {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('runs')).then(load);
  });

  document.getElementById('add').addEventListener('click', function () {
    var w = parseFloat(document.getElementById('w').value);
    var p = parseFloat(document.getElementById('p').value);
    var s = parseFloat(document.getElementById('s').value);
    if (isNaN(w) || isNaN(p) || isNaN(s)) return;
    var next = runs.concat([{
      night: 'Night ' + (runs.length + 1),
      writes: w,
      p99: p,
      stalls: s
    }]);
    ducter.set('runs', next);
    load(next);
    ['w', 'p', 's'].forEach(function (id) { document.getElementById(id).value = ''; });
  });
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"runs":[{"night":"Night 2","writes":181000,"p99":4,"stalls":0},{"night":"Night 3","writes":182000,"p99":4.1,"stalls":0}],"seed":[{"night":"Night 2","writes":181000,"p99":4,"stalls":0},{"night":"Night 3","writes":182000,"p99":4.1,"stalls":0}]}

  [partner message] 已加好合计,放在最上面,字很大。现在一共 ¥1,966,3 笔。

<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">北京出差合计</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="total">¥0</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="count">还没记</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">花销</h2>
<div class="k-card">
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
</div>
<details class="k-add">
  <summary>记一笔</summary>
  <div class="k-field"><label>什么</label><input class="k-input" id="what" placeholder="比如 午饭"></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>金额（元）</label><input class="k-num" id="yuan" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0"></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="2026-11-19"></div>
  <button class="k-btn primary" id="save">记下</button>
</details>
</main>
<script>
function load(){return ducter.get("expenses")||[];}
function draw(){
  const xs=load();
  const total=xs.reduce((s,x)=>s+(Number(x.yuan)||0),0);
  document.getElementById("total").textContent="¥"+total.toLocaleString("zh-CN");
  document.getElementById("count").textContent=xs.length?xs.length+" 笔":"还没记";
  const list=document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML="";
  for(const x of xs){
    const row=document.createElement("div");row.className="k-row";
    const main=document.createElement("div");main.className="k-row-main";main.textContent=x.what;
    const sub=document.createElement("div");sub.className="k-row-sub";sub.textContent=x.day;
    const val=document.createElement("div");val.className="k-row-value";val.textContent="¥"+x.yuan;
    const wrap=document.createElement("div");wrap.appendChild(main);wrap.appendChild(sub);
    row.appendChild(wrap);row.appendChild(val);list.appendChild(row);
  }
}
document.getElementById("save").onclick=function(){
  const what=document.getElementById("what").value.trim();
  const yuan=Number(document.getElementById("yuan").value);
  const day=document.getElementById("day").value.trim();
  if(!what||!yuan)return;
  const xs=load();
  xs.push({what:what,yuan:yuan,day:day||new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)});
  ducter.set("expenses",xs);
  document.getElementById("what").value="";
  document.getElementById("yuan").value="";
  document.getElementById("day").value="";
};
ducter.onChange(function(){draw();ducter.summary("已记 "+load().length+" 笔");});
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}

```

<section class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">今天喝了</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="tot">还没记</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="cnt">今天的第一口，点下面记一笔</div>
</section>

<h2 class="k-h">今天</h2>
<div class="k-card">
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  <div class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>今天还没记，点下面的“记一笔”。</div>
</div>

<details class="k-add" id="add">
  <summary>记一笔</summary>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-field"><label>时间</label><input class="k-input" id="t" placeholder="HH:MM"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label>奶量 (ml)</label><input class="k-num" id="ml" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="例如 120"></div>
    <button class="k-btn primary" id="save">记下</button>
  </div>
</details>

<div class="k-card" id="edit" hidden>
  <div class="k-h">改这一次</div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>时间</label><input class="k-input" id="et" placeholder="HH:MM"></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>奶量 (ml)</label><input class="k-num" id="eml" inputmode="decimal"></div>
  <button class="k-btn primary" id="esave">保存</button>
  <button class="k-btn" id="edel">删除</button>
  <button class="k-btn" id="ecancel">取消</button>
</div>
</main>
<style>
  .k-row { width:100%; disp … dit(i) {
    editIdx = i;
    $('et').value = feeds[i].t;
    $('eml').value = feeds[i].ml;
    $('edit').hidden = false;
  }
  function closeEdit() { editIdx = -1; $('edit').hidden = true; }

  $('list').onclick = function (e) {
    var b = e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('.k-row') : null;
    if (b) openEdit(+b.getAttribute('data-i'));
  };
  $('add').addEventListener('toggle', function () { if (this.open) $('t').value = nowHM(); });
  $('save').onclick = function () {
    var t = normTime($('t').value), m = normMl($('ml').value);
    if (!t || !m) return;
    feeds.push({ d: ymd(new Date()), t: t, ml: m });
    save();
    $('t').value = ''; $('ml').value = '';
    $('add').open = false;
    render();
  };
  $('esave').onclick = function () {
    var t = normTime($('et').value), m = normMl($('eml').value);
    if (!t || !m || editIdx < 0) return;
    feeds[editIdx] = { d: feeds[editIdx].d, t: t, ml: m };
    save(); closeEdit(); render();
  };
  $('edel').onclick = function () {
    if (editIdx < 0) return;
    feeds.splice(editIdx, 1);
    save(); closeEdit(); render();
  };
  $('ecancel').onclick = closeEdit;

  ducter.onChange(function () { pull(); });
  pull();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"d":"2026-11-23","t":"06:10","ml":120},{"d":"2026-11-23","t":"09:05","ml":90}]}

```

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done: the page is on your shelf as "PRs waiting on others", with the four PRs. Tick each one off as it lands and the count updates.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting on others", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1835 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">PRs waiting on others</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="count">4 open</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">Tick each one off when it lands</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">Waiting</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
</main>
<script>
var PRS = ducter.get("prs") || [];
var done = {};

function render() {
  var list = document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML = "";
  var open = 0;
  PRS.forEach(function (p) {
    var isDone = !!done[p.id];
    if (!isDone) open++;
    var row = document.createElement("label");
    row.className = "k-row";
    row.innerHTML =
      '<input type="checkbox" ' + (isDone ? "checked" : "") + ' style="margin-right:10px;width:18px;height:18px">' +
      '<div class="k-row-main" style="' + (isDone ? "text-decoration:line-through;color:var(--d-muted)" : "") + '">' +
      p.repo + " " + p.title + "</div>" +
      '<div class="k-row-sub">' + (isDone ? "Landed" : p.wait) + "</div>";
    row.querySelector("input").addEventListener("change", function (e) {
      done[p.id] = e.target.checked;
      save();
      render();
    });
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  var total = PRS.length - open;
  document.getElementById("count").textContent = open + " open";
  document.getElementById("sub").textContent = total ? total + " landed" : "Tick each one off when it lands";
  ducter.summary(open + " open · " + total + " landed");
}

function save() {
  ducter.set("done", done);
}

Promise.resolve(ducter.get("done")).then(function (v) {
  done = v || {};
  render();
});
if (typeof ducter.onChange === "function") {
  ducter.onChange(function () {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get("done")).then(function (v) {
      done = v || {};
      render();
    });
  });
}
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"id":"ducter-412","repo":"wey-gu/ducter","title":"#412 Compaction cut","wait":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday"},{"id":"mem-10287","repo":"nowledge-co/mem","title":"#10287 Retry on 502","wait":"On CI, failing"},{"id":"hawdb-1592","repo":"nowledge-co/hawdb","title":"#1592 Flaky compaction test","wait":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"ducter-418","repo":"wey-gu/ducter","title":"#418 Apps bar","wait":"Draft"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

--- step 1: user types in the sheet of their app (the first one listed in [apps]), about it: 把「出差花销」换成新样子，能填的先帮我填好
  [partner message] 做好了，「出差花销」换成新样子了，北京出差的高铁和两晚酒店已经填进去，合计 ¥1,698。
  [an app card under the message: changed "出差花销", with Open] the app's file now (v2, 2448 characters): <main class="k-app"><div class="k-sum"><div class="k-sum-label">出差花销合计</div><div class="k-sum-value" id="total"></div><div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub"></div></div><h2 class="k-h">花销明细</h2><div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div><details class="k-add"><summary>记一笔</summary><div class="k-field"><label>出差名称</label><input class="k-input" id="trip"></div><div class="k-field"><label>分类</label><div class="k-chips"><button type="button" class="k-chip" aria-pressed="true" data-cat="交通">交通</button><button type="button" class="k-chip" aria-pressed="false" data-cat="住宿">住宿</button></div></div><div class="k-field"><label>日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="如 11/20"></div><div class="k-field"><label>金额</label><input class="k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal"></div><button class="k-btn primary" id="save">保存</button></details></main><script>
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
let cat="交通";
document.querySelectorAll(".k-chip").forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{cat=b.dataset.cat;document.querySelectorAll(".k-chip").forEach(c=>c.setAttribute("aria-pressed",c===b?"true":"false"))});
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
  const t=xs.reduce((s,x)=>s+Number(x.amt||0),0);
  const tot=$("total");
  if(xs.length){tot.textContent="¥"+t.toLocaleString("zh-CN");tot.classList.remove("k-none");$("sub").textContent=xs.length+" 笔";}
  else{tot.textContent="还没记";tot.classList.add("k-none");$("sub").textContent="记一笔就有合计";}
  const list=$("list");list.textContent="";
  if(!xs.length){const e=document.createElement("div");e.className="k-empty";e.textContent="还没有花销，点下面「记一笔」";list.appendChild(e);return;}
  xs.forEach(x=>{
    const r=document.createElement("div");r.className="k-row";
    const m=document.createElement("div");m.className="k-row-main";m.textContent=x.trip||"未命名";
    const s=document.createElement("div");s.className="k-row-sub";s.textContent=[x.day,x.cat].filter(Boolean).join(" · ");
    m.appendChild(s);
    const v=document.createElement("div");v.className="k-row-value";v.textContent="¥"+Number(x.amt||0).toLocaleString("zh-CN");
    r.appendChild(m);r.appendChild(v);list.appendChild(r);
  });
}
$("save").onclick=()=>{
  const amt=Number($("amt").value);
  if(!amt)return;
  const xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
  xs.push({trip:$("trip").value,amt:amt,cat:cat,day:$("day").value});
  ducter.set("items",xs);
  $("amt").value="";
  draw();
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","amt":662,"cat":"交通","day":"11/20","note":"G1 上海虹桥→北京南 07:00"},{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","amt":1036,"cat":"住宿","day":"11/20–21","note":"亚朵酒店（望京）两晚"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

适合放在空状态里。空白屏幕正好表达"这里还没内容"，柔和的立体质感也比较友好，不会让人觉得冷冰冰。它适合搭配温暖或简约的界面，比如"还没有记录"或"还没有文件"这类提示。

需要注意两点：它只有 320×246，放大显示可能会糊，建议用更大的版本；另外奶油色和深蓝边框如果跟你的主色调差太远，可能显得突兀，可以试着放在实际页面里看看。

```
(earlier) user: 修一下 ~/dev/foo 里 TTL 那个时好时坏的测试
(earlier) partner ack: 我去看看 foo 里那个时好时坏的 TTL 测试，弄清楚了告诉你。  → task "foo: TTL 测试不稳定" done; its report: The TTL test waited on real time for a 1s expiry; under load it fired early. Switched it to a fake clock; 20 runs in a row pass.
(earlier) partner: TTL 测试修好了：它用真实计时去等 1 秒过期，机器一忙就抢跑，现在改成假时钟。
(earlier) user: 再给 ~/dev/foo 的 Cache 加一个 values() 方法
(earlier) partner ack: 我去给 foo 的 Cache 加 values()。  → task "foo: 给 Cache 加 values()" done; its report: Added Cache.values() returning live values; 2 new tests, 14/14 pass.
(earlier) partner: 加好了：values() 只返回没过期的值，加了 2 个测试。
--- step 1: user replies (by swipe, quoting it) to the earlier message with "TTL 测试修好了": 这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻
  [partner ack line] 好，我让 TTL 那个任务再补一条正好到期那一刻的测试，补好了告诉你。
  [sent to task ttl] 用户原话："这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻"

要求：
- 在现有的假时钟 TTL 测试基础上，新增一条测试，覆盖时间正好等于过期时刻（例如 TTL 1 秒，时钟前进到恰好 1 秒）的行为。
- 明确断言这一刻的结果（值是否仍可读、还是已过期），并与你们现有的边界约定保持一致；如果约定本身不清楚，在报告里说明你选了哪种语义以及原因。
- 再前进 1 毫秒（或最小单位）时的行为也要有断言，确保边界两侧都被覆盖。
- 不要改动已有测试的断言；只新增测试。
- 在同一分支上提交，不要合并、不要推送、不要开 PR。
- 完成后报告：新增的测试名称、通过情况（跑的命令和结果），以及你对边界语义的选择。
```
